# Navigation â€” Where Things Live

**Who this is for:** anyone asking "which file do I open to change X?"

This doc is also the **update trigger table**: when you add, move, or rename a
top-level folder or a major module, update the relevant row here in the same
change. That is the mechanism that keeps docs from drifting.

## Top-level map

```
Black-Eclipse-site/
  src/
    app/            Next.js App Router: page.tsx + api/**/route.ts
    components/     UI â€” screens, layout, chat, ui primitives, workspaces
                    (graph-studio/ = the visual workflow editor screens)
    lib/            All logic: data, scraper, agent, domains
                    (graph-studio/ = the canonical Workflow IR + node registry)
                    harness/commands/    = slash-command catalog, parser, router, handlers
                    harness/clients/     = front-door HTTP clients (one -client/-browser pair per surface)
                    harness/data-bridge/ = read-only JDdS/provider capability satellites (entry: harness/data-bridge.ts)
    hooks/          Shared React hooks
    types/          Shared TypeScript types
    generated/      Prisma client output (gitignored â€” do not edit)
  prisma/           schema.prisma (the JDDS schema)
  scripts/          Importers, bridge, doctor, db-guard, QA, local reverse proxy
  skills/           The engineering operating system (ARCHITECT/REMEMBER/â€¦)
  rust/             Native render worker (fframes video generation â€” Phase 1 proof)
  docs/             This folder
  jdds.db           SQLite data vault (untracked runtime seed; doctor.js creates it)
```

At the **repository root**, `README.md` is the single public front door: the
heading, the benchmark block and the install block are all governed by
`docs/public-readme.md` (claim-to-evidence table + copy rules). The only separate
web surface is `site/index.html` — a static, single-file GitHub Pages landing
page (published by `.github/workflows/pages.yml`); it is not served by the app
and has no server, build step, or runtime of its own. `LICENSE` scopes the tree
(MIT, with the `pine-engine/` carve-out), `THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md` and
`sbom.cdx.json` are generated from the lockfiles by `npm run sbom`, and
`SECURITY.md` states the residual risks accepted for the source release.

At the **repository root** (one level above `Black-Eclipse-site/`), there is also
a project skill root:

```
black eclipse/
  .black-eclipse/skills/   Project-carried skills (one folder per skill:
                           skill.json + SKILL.md). Currently the twelve CFA
                           Level I skills â€” `cfa.00` index, one `cfa.01`-`cfa.10`
                           per exam topic (Equities first), and `cfa.99`
                           exam preparation. `CFA-NOTICE.md` records that they
                           are original notes, not affiliated with CFA Institute.
  mobile/                  (planned) Standalone iOS/Android Bot Mode app —
                           Tauri 2 + React 19 + Rust + SQLite. Architecture:
                           docs/mobile/.
  packages/                (planned) Versioned cross-runtime contracts
                           (agent-contracts) shared by mobile and, later, desktop.
```

A project skill root is resolved from the nearest `.git` ancestor, is **untrusted
until an operator records `trust_root`**, and each skill ships inert (`status:
disabled`) until enabled. See `python/harness/skills/project_skills.py` and
`docs/harness/memory-skills-evolution-plan.md`.

doc `docs/trading.md`; ADR-H331/H332/H333/H334/H335/H336. Demo is approval or mandate gated; live additionally requires a real account, Algo Trading, a non-zero magic, a positive `maxMargin`, a time-boxed typed arm and a per-order `LIVE ...` phrase. Unattended live orders require BOTH an armed live connection and a workflow-version-pinned live mandate (maximum 24 h TTL, bounded by symbols/margin/order count); the agent can create neither. Reconciliation never fabricates a fill. Paper execution remains `trading.paper_order`. |

The binding is an APPROVED root **id**, never a path: the run routes forward it as `RunRequest.workspaceRootId`, and `python/harness/code/workspace.py::resolve_workspace(request, root_id)` resolves it through `TrustedRootStore` (`service._trusted_root_by_id`). An unknown/revoked id yields **no** workspace — never a silent fallback to another folder. The chip always shows the folder path it would use (the bound root, or the read-only `defaultRoot` returned by `GET /workspace/roots` / the project route before a chat exists) and renders as a content-sized pill, not a full-width row. A folder chosen before the conversation id exists is stashed in `eclipse.pendingProjectRoot` and bound by the chip when the first message creates the chat. The plan holds the scope/DoD, state machine, contracts, phases, safety floor, testing, rollout and ADR-H217â€“H219 + H222â€“H224 (implemented; H220â€“H221 are reserved by QA Round 1)). Execution constraints: one runtime + one evaluator; candidates run via `execute_run` with an isolated `AgentConfig` (new `evaluation/candidate_invoker.py`); all writes land only through the existing `code/promotion.py` + `code/selfhost.py` single-use operator approval; tests/evaluator/policy/datasets are frozen; no new DB/queue. | Change the ChatBot page look (conversation font, background or wordmark) | Conversation font store `src/lib/chat/appearance.ts` (`be_chat_appearance_v1`, `CHAT_FONT_OPTIONS` = the self-hosted faces + "App font", applied as `--chat-font` on `<html>`, read by `ChatHistory`/`stock-chat.tsx` via `var(--chat-font, var(--font-sans))`), panel `src/components/screens/chatbot-page-settings.tsx` (Customization â€º ChatBot Page), which embeds the backdrop controls below; applied by `useHydrateChatAppearance` in `stock-chat.tsx` |
| Change the page/app background (pattern or wallpaper) | `src/lib/backgrounds.ts` (prefs model + presets + `normalizePresetBackground` + IndexedDB custom image + resolver + `preview`/`previewPrefs` for preset auditioning + the default-on, scheme-aware chat-conversation surface `chatSurface*` â†’ `resolveChatSurfaceCss` + the prompt-bubble fill `chatBubbleColor*` â†’ `resolveChatBubbleCss`), panel `src/components/screens/background-settings.tsx` (Customization â€º ChatBot Page â€º Backdrop & landing wordmark) + `chatbot-page-settings.tsx` (`ChatBackgroundCard`), layer CSS in `src/app/globals.css` (`body`/`.app-page-bg` pattern, `.app-bg-layer` wallpaper filling the main panel, `.chat-surface-layer` conversation surface, `.chat-bubble` prompt fill), applied by `useHydrateBackground` in `stock-chat.tsx` + `refreshBackground()` after theme writes; shipped art in `public/backgrounds/` |
| Change API protection / rate limits | `src/lib/api-guard.ts` |
| Add a data provider / work on connectors | `src/lib/connectors/` (`catalog`, `types`, `registry`, `resolver`, `custom-rest`, `security`, `secrets`, `mapping`, `refresh`, `store`); settings UI `src/components/screens/data-sources-panel.tsx` |
| Add/change a provider API key | `src/lib/provider-keys.ts` (`PROVIDER_KEYS` registry + encrypted vault) â†’ `/api/provider-keys` â†’ `src/components/screens/provider-registry-panel.tsx` (`ProviderRegistry`, filtered by the `home` prop; `ProviderKeyEditor` is the single-key form); LLM keys render under `settings.ai`, financial/data keys under `settings.investor-eye`. An LLM provider also needs ONE preset id in `src/lib/agent/presets.ts` matching a `ProviderSpec` id in `python/harness/models.py` `PROVIDER_CATALOG` (the runtime rejects an unknown id) |
| Change Settings information architecture / sections | `src/components/screens/settings.tsx` (`SectionId`, `NAV`), sub-pages in `src/lib/search/registry.ts` (`settings.*`) |
| Explain why a provider exists / which feature uses it | `src/lib/providers/directory.ts` (`providerMeta`, `INVESTOR_EYE_FEATURES`) â†’ registry UI `src/components/screens/provider-registry-panel.tsx` |
| Test an LLM connection | `POST /api/agent/test` (real endpoint request) |
| Change the model picker / declared model list | `python/harness/models.py` (`PROVIDER_MODELS`, exposed via `list_providers()` â†’ `/api/harness/models`), client hook `src/lib/agent/model-catalog.ts` (`useModelCatalog`, `modelsForProvider`), UI Settings â€º AI / LLM (`src/components/screens/settings.tsx`, Model field datalist + context-window hint) |
| Pull/ingest custom dataset records | `PUT /api/datasets` â†’ `pullDataset` in `src/lib/connectors/refresh.ts` |
| Scheduled connector refresh | `src/lib/connectors/refresh-scheduler.ts` (started in `src/instrumentation.ts`) |
| Change the DB schema | `prisma/schema.prisma` â†’ `prisma db push` |
| Change the DB client | `src/lib/db.ts` |
| Change boot behavior / start a worker | `src/instrumentation.ts` |
| Change styling / glass classes | `src/app/globals.css` (see the `black-eclipse-ui` skill) |
| Change boot diagnostics | `scripts/doctor.js`, `scripts/up.js` |
| Change DB durability/backup | `scripts/db-guard.js` |

## By domain folder (`src/lib/`)

| Folder | Owns |
|---|---|
| `agent/` | Provider abstraction, tool loop, multi-agent research, journal tools |
| `src/lib/harness/` | Harness **front door**: input validation, vault/agent resolution, Python-runtime client + launcher, read-only JDdS data bridge |
| `python/harness/` (repo root) | The **agentic runtime**: agents, tools, model providers, LangGraph loop, events, `agent-memory.db` persistence |
| `alerts/` | Condition evaluator, feeds, notifier, alert monitor |
| `aurora/` | Aurora customization: sentiment palette + motion/shape config + render styles (ribbon/dots/scanlines/pillars/blocks) (`config.ts`, `be_aurora_config_v2`), zustand store (`store.ts`), presets/validation (`theme.ts`) |
| `theme/` | Centralized customization: `EclipseTheme` model incl. `scheme: 'dark' \| 'light'` + presets (incl. white-background `White`/`Paper`/`Snow`/`Ivory`; `ThemePreset` carries an optional partial `background` snapshot so a preset restyles the ChatBot landing too) + brand-color generator + derived-color resolution + validation (`tokens.ts`), persisted zustand store + owns the `dark` class + scheme-scoped CSS-token injection + user presets w/ chart palette AND backdrop (`store.ts`, incl. `applyPreset(theme, background)` + `refreshBackground()`), chart-palette bridge + preview (`bridge.ts`) |
| `backgrounds.ts` (`lib/`) | App background: `image/solid` prefs model (wallpaper, or flat/gradient/pattern fill + fully tunable wordmark) + the default-on scheme-aware chat-conversation surface (`chatSurfaceEnabled/Fill/Color/ColorLight/Color2/Opacity`, `resolveChatSurfaceCss`) + the prompt-bubble fill (`chatBubbleColor/chatBubbleColorLight`, `resolveChatBubbleCss`) + shipped wallpaper catalog + IndexedDB custom-image storage + browser-side downscale + CSS resolver/store (`useBackgroundStore`, `useHydrateBackground`, non-persisted `preview`/`previewPrefs` for preset auditioning, `normalizePresetBackground`) |
| `chart/` | Chart types, data hooks, indicators, drawings, structure, providers |
| `connectors/` | Universal Data Connector Layer: provider catalog, capability resolver, custom REST connectors, SSRF/secret security, custom datasets, scheduled refresh |
| `provider-keys.ts` (`lib/`) | The API-key vault: registry of every operator credential + AES-GCM encrypted storage + env resolution/apply |
| `navigation-history.ts` (`lib/`) | Capped in-memory tab-visit stack (`recordVisit`/`backTarget`) backing `Alt+â†`; framework-free, no React |
| `providers/` | Provider directory: purpose / used-by / tier metadata + the Investor Eye featureâ†’provider map (client-safe) |
| `dashboard/` | Widget catalog/definitions, renderers, market views, intelligence, dashboard + portfolio stores |
| `i18n/` | Interface localization: locale registry + RTL, dictionaries, translator, persisted language/accessibility store |
| `economics/` | Macro fetching, sources, snapshot, search-based official-page fallback, last-known-good cache, the calendar (Fincept mirror + official FOMC/BLS/BEA/Census/ECB/BoE/BoJ schedules), the market-wide EDGAR filings feed and full-text search |
| `fundamentals/` | Investor financial-analysis tier: provider abstraction (SEC/FMP/Yahoo), Eclipse schema normalization, derived metrics + data-driven risks, JDDS cache â€” see `ARCHITECTURE.md` â€º Fundamentals |
| `investor-eye/` | World-state tier: event sources (GDELT/USGS/FIRMS/news), normalization/clustering, map layers, source governance catalog, geographic boundary lookup, risk/exposure/transmission analytics, ingestion + cache |
| `journal/` | Journal data access, trade math, analytics suite, CSV import, broker projection (`broker/`) |
| `market-workspace/` | The market-panel workspace engine |
| `news/` | Background RSS ingestion |
| `portfolio/` | Portfolio types + risk math |
| `scraper/` | Price/universe scraping, scheduler, fetchers, CSV |
| `search/` | Global search registry, intent, query grammar/operators, ranking, resolver, calculator/commands, stores |
| `screener/` | Market-screener shared logic: instrument record types + zero-valued demo/placeholder rows (`data.ts`), price/cap/time formatting (`format.ts`). Consumed by the screener UI **and** `/api/stocks` |
| `sidebar/` | Sidebar config + store |
| `sounds/` | Terminal-style UI audio feedback: cue catalogue, Howler manager, persisted pref store, delegated listener provider |
| `workspace/` | The widget workspace kernel (DataHub, ops, persistence, canvas) |

## First-run setup and reset

| I want to… | Open |
|---|---|
| Change the onboarding questions, the included/connected/advanced split, or the commercial-use matrix | `src/lib/setup/source-catalog.ts` (the one source registry), `src/lib/setup/capabilities.ts` (capability grouping), `src/lib/setup/bundles.ts`, `src/lib/setup/defaults.ts`; plan `docs/onboarding-plan.md` |
| Change the setup screen (welcome/theme/AI/data/review) | `src/components/setup/onboarding-gate.tsx` (mount + fetch + first-run decision handoff), `onboarding-shell.tsx` (step machine + bounded finish), `steps/` (incl. `theme-step.tsx`), `parts/`; gate mounted in `src/app/layout.tsx`; `src/app/page.tsx` withholds the app shell while setup is pending |
| Change what onboarding persists or how an upgraded install is detected | `src/lib/setup/state.ts` (`onboarding_v1`, `data_source_prefs_v1`), `src/lib/setup/existing-install.ts`, `src/lib/setup/gate.ts` (server-side first-run decision used by `app/page.tsx`); API `src/app/api/setup/route.ts` |
| Change the readiness check | `src/lib/setup/check.ts`, `src/app/api/setup/check/route.ts` |
| Enforce a source toggle in the fetch path | `src/lib/data-source-preferences.ts`; consumers `src/lib/scraper/fetchers/index.ts`, `src/lib/connectors/providers/crypto.ts`, `src/app/api/economics/route.ts` |
| Re-run onboarding or reset Eclipse | Settings → General ("Run setup again"), Settings → Data & Storage ("Reset setup", "Factory reset"), `src/app/api/setup/reset/route.ts`, `scripts/lib/reset.js`, `scripts/reset.js`, `bin/black.js` (`black onboarding`, `black reset --onboarding|--setup|--factory`), boot hook `scripts/doctor.js`, primitive `scripts/wipe-personal-data.js` |
| Read the reset scopes and safety rules | `docs/onboarding-plan.md` §13; `docs/data-and-privacy.md` |
| Check which data sources are included, personal-use, or commercial-safe | `docs/data-sources.md` (generated from `src/lib/setup/source-catalog.ts`; run `npm run docs:data-sources`) |

## Research reports

- `docs/harness/roadmap/w8-observability-and-slos-plan.md` — the implemented W8
  plan: the signal dictionary for every workstream (model attempts/TTFT,
  context pressure/compression, tool batches/guardrails, goals, skill
  execution, run-kernel integrity, delegation/file checkpoints), eager
  objectives with an honest `unmeasured` state, SLO vs invariant semantics,
  report schema v2, the data-driven release gate, real Prometheus rules,
  twelve conformance cases, slices W8.0–W8.7 and exit criteria.
- `docs/harness/roadmap/w7-delegation-and-file-safety-plan.md` — the complete
  W7 plan: Fabric-backed `delegate_async`/status/result/cancel/wait, the
  `output_schema` + one-repair contract, step-boundary steering, per-child
  worktrees, the shadow-git checkpoint store, `/rollback` and the restore
  approval, slices W7.0–W7.6, flags, fault matrix and rollout.
- `docs/harness/roadmap/w6-run-kernel-integrity-plan.md` — the complete W6 plan:
  fenced progress writes, atomic checkpoint + message boundaries, pre-dispatch
  idempotency states, durable per-run event sequences, the lease-aware
  watchdog, the command hardline, shell sandbox parity and the staged
  effect-token enforce rollout.
- `docs/harness/roadmap/w5-learning-loop-plan.md` — the complete W5 plan:
  behavioural skill execution eval and run-id-gated promotion, the skill curator
  (dry_run → propose → apply, inert drafts only), BM25 hybrid memory retrieval
  behind `HARNESS_MEMORY_HYBRID`, and lesson-feedback verification.
- `docs/harness/roadmap/w3-tool-execution-plan.md` — the complete W3 plan:
  contiguous read-only batches, success-signature guardrails, BM25/tiered
  disclosure, concurrency audit, slices and fault matrix.
- `docs/harness/roadmap/w2-context-economics-plan.md` — the complete W2 plan:
  usage anchor, 50/70/85 gates, prune/summary/skeleton ladder, prompt-cache
  tiers, any-step overflow recovery, slices and fault matrix.
- `docs/harness/roadmap/w1-model-gateway-plan.md` — the complete W1 plan:
  candidate planning, retry/fallback matrix, credential pool, first-token
  watchdog, per-attempt accounting, slices and fault matrix.
- `docs/harness/baselines/w2-8f79bed4-2026-11-12.json` — the W2 context-economics baseline (same schema as W0/W1; 74/74 terminal).
- `docs/harness/baselines/w1-8f79bed4-2026-11-12.json` — the W1 gateway
  baseline (same schema as W0; 74/74 terminal).
- `docs/harness/baselines/w0-0a7ec906-2026-11-12.json` — the first W0 baseline
  (read-only soak + gate verdicts; unknown stays unknown).
- `docs/harness/roadmap/w0-flags-and-review.md` — the flag matrix, kill
  switches and "no second seam" reviewer checklist.
- `docs/harness/roadmap/w0-baseline-and-guardrails-plan.md` — the detailed W0
  plan: metric dictionary, baseline artifact schema, measurement protocol,
  flag registry, kill-switch tests, "no second seam" review and day-by-day
  exit criteria.
- `docs/harness/roadmap/hermes-inspired-improvement-plan.md` — the detailed,
  slice-by-slice engineering plan for closing the Hermes gap: model gateway,
  context anchors/compression ladder, tool execution (parallel, guardrailed,
  discoverable), autonomy, learning loop, run-kernel hardening, delegation and
  file checkpoints. Read this when the
  question is "what exactly do we change, in what order, and how do we test it".
- `docs/research/hermes-vs-eclipse/` — read-only architectural comparison of
  Eclipse against NousResearch/hermes-agent (2026-11-12). Start with
  `EXECUTIVE-SUMMARY.md`; the subsystem evidence is in
  `ARCHITECTURE-COMPARISON.md`, the copy/adapt/avoid decisions in
  `ADOPTION-ANALYSIS.md`, the proposed design in `TARGET-ARCHITECTURE.md`, the
  prioritized tasks in `IMPLEMENTATION-ROADMAP.md`, and the status ledger in
  `FEATURE-GAP-MATRIX.md`. This is research, not a shipped contract; a proposal
  in these files needs its own ADR before it changes production code.

## Retired / do not build on

- `src/lib/customize/` â€” superseded customization kernel (ADR-005).
- The old JDdS daemon and its PostgreSQL provider system â€” removed.
- `MarketSidePanel` as a separate page â€” its widgets are now the workspace right
  zone (legacy name: "rail").
